# ui cleanup report

Phase 7; base `6d2a5867`.

The original 26 tracked UI files supply desktop and tray clients, canonical text/draft transport, settings and several unwired experimental surfaces. Production launch flows from sofia.__main__ or sofia.ui.__main__ into desktop worker/controller and the canonical SofiaApplication; tray_launcher starts tray_agent, which consumes Windows tray/settings and service controls.

| Original file | Decision | Responsibility and usage evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `__init__.py` | KEEP | PKG-UI text-first and workbench primitives. Bounded package API or module entry point; local definitions and package exports do not by themselves establish production execution. |
| `__main__.py` | KEEP | Launch the local PKG-UI desktop workbench. Bounded package API or module entry point; local definitions and package exports do not by themselves establish production execution. |
| `assets/sofia_fox.ico` | KEEP | Native Windows tray icon resource loaded by windows_tray; packaged through setuptools asset configuration. |
| `control_center.py` | KEEP | Tray/master-settings control contracts for the Sofía desktop client. Imported by `ui/desktop_application.py`, `ui/fleet_service_control.py`, `ui/remote_client.py`, `ui/service_control.py`, `ui/settings_window.py`, `ui/tray_agent.py`, `ui/windows_tray.py`. |
| `delivery.py` | DELETE | Caller-reported renderer acknowledgments have no runtime, desktop, voice or avatar consumer; only package exports and own tests. |
| `desktop.py` | KEEP | Low-resource Windows desktop shell for Sofía. Imported by `__main__.py`, `ui/__main__.py`. |
| `desktop_application.py` | KEEP | Canonical desktop application composition. Imported by `ui/desktop_worker.py`. |
| `desktop_controller.py` | KEEP | Application-facing controller for the Windows text workbench. Imported by `ui/desktop_worker.py`. |
| `desktop_worker.py` | KEEP | Single-owner worker thread for the desktop Sofía client. Imported by `ui/desktop.py`. |
| `drafts.py` | KEEP | Persistent unsent draft state for PKG-UI text clients. Imported by `application/bootstrap.py`, `ui/text.py`. |
| `fleet_service_control.py` | DELETE | Unconstructed bridge has no callers or tests; live tray local controls still fail closed on remote requests. |
| `process_lock.py` | KEEP | Cross-platform lifetime lock for one Sofía tray agent per state database. Imported by `ui/tray_agent.py`, `ui/tray_launcher.py`. |
| `quick_tools.py` | KEEP | Curated desktop quick-tool prompts. Imported by `ui/desktop.py`. |
| `remote_client.py` | DELETE | Explicitly deferred endpoint selector has no production caller; desktop already normalizes old remote settings to local mode. |
| `remote_transport.py` | DELETE | Deferred remote chat server/client and its independent request ledger are unwired. Preserve actual Fleet mTLS and Discord transport in their owners. |
| `runtime_authority.py` | DELETE | No publisher or consumer exists for this competing runtime-chat authority record. |
| `service_control.py` | KEEP | Typed service/model controls used by the tray client. Imported by `ui/fleet_service_control.py`, `ui/tray_agent.py`. |
| `settings_window.py` | KEEP | Tkinter master settings window for the Sofía desktop/tray client. Bounded package API or module entry point; local definitions and package exports do not by themselves establish production execution. |
| `terminal.py` | KEEP | Tracked asset. Imported by `__main__.py`. |
| `text.py` | KEEP | Text-first PKG-UI adapter over Sofía's canonical conversation service. Imported by `application/bootstrap.py`, `ui/desktop_controller.py`. |
| `theme.py` | KEEP | Bounded adaptive desktop theming from trusted Sofía state. Imported by `ui/desktop.py`, `ui/desktop_controller.py`, `ui/desktop_worker.py`. |
| `tray_agent.py` | KEEP | Standalone Windows tray control process for Sofía. Bounded package API or module entry point; local definitions and package exports do not by themselves establish production execution. |
| `tray_launcher.py` | KEEP | Production launcher for the singleton Windows tray agent. Imported by `__main__.py`. |
| `windows_startup.py` | KEEP | Settings window invokes registration; tray startup command builds package bootstrap. Test now simulates python.exe explicitly and separately verifies pythonw preference. |
| `windows_tray.py` | KEEP | Native Windows notification-area renderer for Sofía. Imported by `ui/tray_agent.py`. |
| `workbench.py` | DELETE | Headless page/notebook/binder store is never constructed by the desktop controller; only exported and tested. Desktop chat uses canonical text UI and drafts. |

Removed six unwired modules and five tests exclusively exercising them; repaired package exports and historical documentation/acceptance commands. The startup implementation was valid: the test accidentally asserted Windows executable suffixes against Linux sys.executable. It now supplies a deterministic Windows executable and verifies both fallback and existing windowless runner, retaining command/bootstrap assertions. Large desktop/settings modules remain cohesive view/controller forms; distinct state, service authorization and transport boundaries remain separate. Old remote-chat settings columns/types remain required by the live migration to local mode; deleting them would break saved settings. No unsupported renderer/remote-chat behavior was wired to justify retention.

## Validation and remaining scope

Compile passed. UI/application/avatar/conversation gate: 353 passed. Full suite: 3,418 passed, 7 failed, 2 skipped (64.83s). Five failures require unavailable Ollama; two diagnosed path/platform cases remain assigned to filesystem/config. The Windows startup failure is fixed. Source/test search for retired module imports returned no matches; diff whitespace check passed. Tests deleted are prototype-only cases, not newly skipped cases.

Canonical SofiaApplication/conversation storage owns chat; UIDraftStore owns only unsent client drafts. DesktopControlSettingsStore owns tray preferences. Runtime stays local to the configured canonical database. Native Tk/Windows rendering and service controls were validated by mocked/source tests, not live Windows canaries.

Checkpoint: `git log -1 --format=%H -- docs/development/ui-cleanup-report.md`.

## Definition, import and reference inventory

Reference candidates below are lexical evidence, not a resolved Python call graph. Each method is listed; receiver identity, callbacks, package exports and module-local calls require inspection. Production paths and intentional offline APIs are identified above. Existing state rows/files belonging to retired prototypes are not deleted by source cleanup.
